Business Ethics and Compliance
Nanjing Sinolife has consistently regarded ethics compliance as the 
cornerstone o f our business development, thereby strictly abiding by laws and 
regulations, continuously improving the corporate governance mechanism,  
improving the internal management systems, and vigorously promoting a  
culture of integrity. Furthermore, we consistently require ourselves and our  
par tners to uphold the highest standards of business ethics, striving to create 
an honest, upright, and proactive team environment.
We resolutely implement a “zero tolerance ” policy towards all forms of
  
commercial c orruption, and explicitly prohibit any form of bribery, corruption, 
or improper quid pro quo arrangements. In addition, we resolutely reject any  
acts o f market monopolization, price manipulation, or false advertising, actively 
advocate f or industry self-regulation, and work with our partners to maintain a 
healthy m
arket order, promoting the sustainable development of the industry. 
We abide by various laws and regulations in the jurisdictions where we
  
operate, including the Law Against Unfair Competition of the People’s Republic  
of China , the Anti-Money Laundering Law of the People ’s Republic of China , 
and t he Certain Opinions of the State Council on Promoting Fair Competition 
in t he Market  and Maintaining the Normal Order of the Market , and formulate 
internal management system norms such as the Employee Code of Conduct 
and Code of Business Conduct . Our robust compliance management system 
has been established to integrate the requirements of anti-corruption, anti-
monopoly, and anti-unfair competition throughout the entire business process,  
ensuring t hat every business activity is in compliance with legal and regulatory 
requirements as well as commercial ethical standards.
The Group has established comprehensive monitoring and whistleblowing  
mechanisms to address breaches of business ethics, ensuring that relevant  
issues are promptly and properly handled. We maintain accessible internal  
and external reporting channels to encourage employees and partners to  
actively disclose potential risks and misconduct. Employees may also report  
any significant incident of corruption directly to the management, enabling  
the Group to promptly assign dedicated personnel to conduct investigations,  
evidence collection and verification. Upon completion of the investigation  
and verification, the Group takes impartial actions on the reported content  
in accordance with regulations and guidelines, and reports and rectifies  
internally, forming a complete closed-loop management. At the same time,  
we h ave established a rigorous whistleblower protection mechanism, applying 
anonymous p rotection to the whistleblower ’s i dentity throughout the process 
to e nsure their information is strictly kept confidential. If whistleblowers suffer 
retaliation or unfair treatment due to their whistleblowing actions and their
  
legitimate rights and interests are impaired, they have the right to request  
the Group’s management to promptly take necessary protective measures in  
accordance with the relevant protection systems.
商業道德與合規
南京中生始終將遵守道德作為企業發展的基
石，嚴 格 遵 守 法 律 法 規
 ，不 斷 健 全 企 業 管 治
機 制 ，完 善 內 部 管 理 制 度，大 力 推 行 廉 潔 文
化，始終以最高的商業道德標準要求自身及
合 作 夥 伴
，致 力 創 造 出 一 個 廉 潔、清 正、積
極進取的團隊環境 。
我們對待所有形式的商業腐敗行為堅決實行
「零 容 忍 」 政 策，明 確 禁 止 任 何 形 式 的 貪 污、
賄 賂 和 利 益 輸 送 行 為 。此 外，我 們 堅 決 杜 絕
市 場 壟 斷 、價 格 操 縱、虛 假 宣 傳 等 行 為，積
極倡導行業自律
 ，與合作夥伴共同維護健康
的 市 場 秩 序 ，推 動 行 業 的 可 持 續 發 展。我 們
嚴格遵守 《 中華人民共和國反不正當競爭法》
《中華人民共和國反洗錢法 》 《國務院關於促進
市場公平競爭維護市場正常秩序的若干意見 》
等運營所在地的各項法律法規 ，並制定 《員工
守則 》 和 《商業準則》 等內部管理制度規範，通
過 建 立 健 全 的 合 規 管 理 體 系
 ，將 反 貪 腐、反
壟斷和反不正當競爭的要求貫穿於業務全流
程，確保每一項經營活動都符合法律法規和
商業道德標準 。
本集團針對違反商業道德的行為已建立完善
的監督與舉報機制
 ，確保相關問題能及時妥
善 處 理 。我 們 暢 通 內 外 部 舉 報 渠 道，鼓 勵 員
工及合作夥伴積極反饋潛在風險與違規行為
 。
員工亦可直接向管理層舉報重大貪污腐敗事
件，以便集團迅速安排專人展開調查取證與
核 實 。經 調 查 核 實 後，集 團 依 規 依 紀 對 舉 報
內 容 作 出 公 正 處 理
 ，並 在 內 部 通 報 整 改，形
成 完 整 的 管 理 閉 環 。同 時，我 們 設 有 嚴 謹 的
舉報人保護機制 ，全程對舉報人身份進行匿
名 化 處 理 ，確 保 其 信 息 嚴 格 保 密。若 舉 報 人
因舉報行為遭受打擊報復或不公正對待
 ，其
合法權益受損時 ，有權要求集團管理層依據
相關保護制度及時採取必要保護措施 。

Anti-Corruption and Culture of Integrity
The Group actively cultivates a culture of integrity within the Company, 
comprehensively enhancing employees ’ compliance awareness and business  
ethics standards through regular and institutionalized educational activities. We  
arrange anti-corruption and compliance training at the onboarding stage of  
new h ires to assist them in establishing correct values and professional ethics. 
At the same time, we hold anti-corruption themed promotions and training
  
for d irectors and employees from time to time to continuously strengthen the 
integrity awareness and foster a work environment of honesty and integrity.
  
During the Reporting Period, we conducted special training on integrity for  
core senior management of the e-commerce business. The training focused  
on c ommon integrity risks within the e-commerce sector, with pre-prevention 
as the core objective, and systematically reviewed integrity standards and
  
compliance management requirements for each role, aiming to strengthen  
the integrity risk prevention and control system of the e-commerce business  
line a nd promote healthy and sustainable business development. Through the 
training, t
he Group not only reiterated its zero-tolerance stance on corruption 
behaviors, but also effectively enhanced employees ’ recognition and
  
participation in anti-corruption work, improving the overall ability to prevent  
and crack down on corrupt behaviors.
We continue to strengthen the framework of integrity and ethical operations.  
Through internal systems and regulations such as the Employee Code of 
Conduct  and Code of Business Conduct , we clearly define the standards of 
conduct that employees must follow in their dealings with customers, partners,

THRIVING WITH NATURE, SAFEGUARDING OUR SHARED 
HOME
In full recognition of the severe challenges brought by climate change, the 
Group actively assumes environmental responsibilities, upholds the green  
development concept, and is committed to implementing environmental  
sustainability principles in its business operations, thereby continuously  
protecting n atural resources and the ecosystem. We continue to optimize our 
environmental management system, explore new green operation models,  
and promote the development of a circular economy. Through these efforts,  
we strive to operate as an environmentally friendly enterprise, and actively  
participate in global climate governance, reducing our environmental impact  
through practical actions.
The Group has established a strict environmental compliance management  
system, ensuring full compliance with applicable local environmental  
protection l aws, regulations, and requirements in all operating regions. We are 
in compliance with the following major laws and regulations, including but not 
limited t o, the Environmental Protection Law of the People ’s R epublic of China, 
the L aw of the People ’s R epublic of China on Prevention and Control of Water 
Pollution, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Solid Waste Pollution to  
the Environment of the People ’s Republic of China, the Law of the People ’s 
Republic o f China on Prevention and Control of Pollution from Environmental 
Noise, a nd the New Zealand Environment Act, 1986, as well as environmental 
laws, regulations and ordinances of the places where we operate.
On this basis, we establish and continuously improve our environmental
  
management system, thereby establishing an environmental protection  
taskforce t o comprehensively coordinate emissions compliance management, 
resource conservation, energy-saving and emission-reduction initiatives,  
and many other areas. To ensure the effective operation of the system, we  
regularly a ssess our environmental management performance, and implement 
targeted i
mprovements based on the assessment results, thereby continuously 
elevating our ecological environmental protection management standard.
  
At the same time, we have formulated and implemented a series of internal  
management documents such as the Corporate Environmental Management  
System, the Energy Conservation Management System, the Corporate Solid  
Waste M anagement System, the Precautionary Measures for Hazardous Waste, 
and the Wastewater Management System, providing institutional safeguards
  
for the standardized execution of our environmental management.
We have issued a “Green Initiative” and established the “5R” principles for our  
environmental offices, internalizing the green concept into employees ’ code  
of c onduct at the institutional level. At the same time, we continue to promote 
environmental p rotection practices, effectively promoting the green culture to 
take root and continuously deepen within the Company.

Environmental, Social and Governance Report

Emissions and Waste Management
The Group continues to enhance the refinement of our environmental 
management throughout the entire production and operation process, by  
stepping up investments in the environmental protection, and adopting  
multiple measures to continuously reduce the environmental impact of its  
production a nd operations. While ensuring that 100% of all types of emissions 
are d isposed in a compliant manner, we actively promote the implementation 
of various emission reduction measures, and extensively explore innovative
  
pathways for waste recycling and reuse. During the Reporting Period, the  
Group did not experience any environmental violations related to excessive  
pollutants o r illegal discharge, earnestly fulfilling the principal responsibility for 
our environmental protection.
Exhaust Gas and Greenhouse Gas Management
Based on product characteristics, the Group ’s production process only  
generates a small amount of experimental exhaust gas in the laboratory  
stage, w hile other stages basically achieve zero exhaust gas emissions without 
causing e
nvironmental pollution. Based on the materiality principle of the ESG 
Report, taking into account that the emission volume of such exhaust gas is
  
extremely l ow and its environmental impact is negligible, we did not disclose 
relevant emission data and detailed information.
During the Reporting Period, the Group conducted comprehensive statistics
  
and sorting of greenhouse gas emissions in each scope in strict compliance  
with greenhouse gas emission accounting standards. Among them, Scope  
I direct greenhouse gases emissions are relatively controllable, as their  
emission sources are mainly concentrated in two core scenarios: one is the  
fuel consumption generated by the Group ’s own transportation vehicles  
during daily operations, where the emissions fluctuate reasonably with the  
volume of transportation tasks. The other is the emissions generated from  
the consumption of liquefied petroleum gas and natural gas used for food  
processing, heating, etc. during the operation of the Group ’s own canteens.  
Scope II indirect greenhouse gas emissions are the main source of indirect  
emissions in the Group ’s operation process, primarily coming from the  
consumption o f purchased electricity in all operating sites such as production, 
offices, and laboratories, where the emission amount is positively correlated  
with t he Group’s o verall electricity consumption. In addition, Scope III indirect 
greenhouse gas emissions mainly originate from air travel by Group employees 
for business trips, where the emissions vary reasonably according to annual  
travel plans and business needs, remaining overall within a manageable range.
排放與廢棄物管理
本集團持續提升生產運營全過程的環境管理
精 細 化 水 平 ，加 大 環 保 投 入 力 度，多 措 並 舉
持續降低生產經營對環境的影響 。我們在確
保各類排放物 1 00% 合規處置的基礎上，積極
推進各項減排措施落地 ，並深入探索廢棄物
回 收 再 利 用 的 創 新 途 徑 。報 告 期 內，本 集 團
未發生任何污染物超標 、違規排放等環保違
規事件 ，切實履行環境保護主體責任。
廢氣及溫室氣體管理
本集團基於產品特性，生產工藝流程中僅實
驗室環節會產生少量實驗廢氣 ，其餘環節基
本 實 現 廢 氣 零 排 放 ，不 會 對 環 境 造 成 污 染。
基於 E SG 報告重要性原則，考慮到該類廢氣排
放 量 極 低 、對 環 境 影 響 可 忽 略 不 計，故 不 披
露相關排放數據及詳細信息 。
報告期內 ，本集團嚴格按照溫室氣體排放核
算標準
，對各範圍溫室氣體排放情況進行全
面 統 計 與 梳 理
 。其 中，範 圍 一 溫 室 氣 體 直 接
排放量相對可控 ，其排放來源主要集中於兩
大核心場景 ：一是集團自有運輸工具在日常
運營過程中產生的燃油消耗 ，該部分排放隨
運輸任務量合理波動 ；二是集團自有食堂運
營 過 程 中 ，用 於 食 材 加 工、供 暖 等 環 節 的 液
化石油氣及天然氣消耗所產生的排放
 。範圍
二溫室氣體間接排放為集團運營過程中的主
要 間 接 排 放 來 源 ，核 心 來 自 生 產、辦 公、實
驗室等所有經營場所外購電力的消耗 ，其排
放 量 與 集 團 整 體 用 電 量 呈 正 相 關 。此 外，範
圍三溫室氣體間接排放主要來源於集團員工
因公出差產生的航空差旅
 ，該部分排放根據
年度差旅計劃及業務需求合理變動 ，整體處
於可管控範圍 。

環境、社會及管治報告
Hazardous Waste Management
To mitigate potential impacts on the surrounding environment, the Group  
implements strict controls across the entire process of hazardous waste,  
from generation, storage, and transportation to disposal. Our relevant  
waste is stored according to the standards of the places where we operate.  
Waste management processes are established to ensure that aspects like  
classification, labelling, and record-keeping comply with relevant laws and  
regulations. During the disposal stage, we hand them over to qualified third-
party institutions for proper handling, with strict supervision to minimize  
pollution risks to the greatest extent. We did not record any hazardous waste  
disposal for this year.
Non-hazardous Waste Management
To recycle resources, the Group comprehensively implements waste  
classification in all office buildings and kitchens within the industry park, to  
facilitate t he recycling and reuse of non-hazardous waste. The non-hazardous 
waste generated from our operations mainly includes packaging waste,  
personal protective equipment waste, electronic waste, office waste, and  
kitchen waste. All waste undergoes centralized collection, identification,  
classification, and labeling procedures before being temporarily stored in  
dedicated waste bins. During this process, we strictly implement protective  
measures such as preventing leakage and fly-away, eliminating pollution to  
soil and land. For recyclables, we prioritize resource recovery processing. As  
for non-recyclables such as kitchen waste and domestic waste, we uniformly  
entrust municipal departments for transportation and disposal.
During the Reporting Period, our waste emissions are as follows:
有害廢棄物管理
為降低對周邊環境的潛在影響 ，本集團對有
害 廢 棄 物 的 產 生 、儲 存、運 輸 及 處 置 等 全 流
程實施嚴格管控 。我們的相關廢棄物均依據
運營所在地標準設定貯存條件 ，並制定廢棄
物 管 理 流 程 ，確 保 分 類、標 籤 及 記 錄 等 環 節
符 合 相 關 法 律 法 規 。在 處 置 環 節，我 們 則 交
予具備資質的第三方機構進行妥善處理 ，嚴
格監督以最大限度降低污染風險 。本年度我
們沒有記錄任何有害廢棄物處置情況 。
無害廢棄物管理
為實現資源的循環利用 ，本集團於園區內所
有辦公樓及廚房全面推行垃圾分類 ，以便於
無害廢棄物的回收與再利用 。我們產生的無
害 廢 棄 物 主 要 涵 蓋 包 裝 廢 料 、勞 保 廢 棄 物、
電 子 廢 棄 物 、辦 公 垃 圾 及 廚 餘 垃 圾 等。所 有
廢 棄 物 均 經 由 集 中 收 集 、鑑 別、分 類 及 貼 標
程序後 ，暫存於專用廢棄箱中。在此過程中，
我 們 嚴 格 落 實 防 泄 漏 、防 飛 揚 等 防 護 措 施，
杜絕對土壤及土地環境的污染 。針對可回收
物，我 們 優 先 進 行 資 源 化 處 理 ；而 對 於 廚 餘
垃圾及生活垃圾等不可回收物 ，則統一委託
市政部門進行清運處置 。

Wastewater Management
The Group ’s wastewater mainly originates from domestic sewage generated  
during daily operations, while the production stage achieves full recycling  
and reuse through a biological recycling system. We are committed to  
reducing wastewater discharge to minimize the impact on the surrounding  
ecological environment, and formulate internal management guidelines in  
strict c ompliance with applicable environmental regulations in each operating 
region, t hereby establishing the environmental target of “100% u p-to-standard 
discharge of wastewater”.
To ensure that the entire process of wastewater from source to discharge  
is under control, we strengthen water intensity control for the production  
stage, preventing the generation of new pollutants from the source. We  
have established a hierarchical control system for wastewater management,  
implementing discharge management responsibilities on a departmental  
basis, and ensuring that emission reduction measures are in place through real-
time dynamic monitoring of the water usage process, setting key monitoring  
indicators, and establishing a traceability mechanism. At the same time, we  
conduct regular evaluations and entrust third-party institutions to perform  
compliance testing to verify the effectiveness of our management.

corresponding wastewater discharge volume has fluctuated and increased.

 。我們致力於削減廢水排放量，

 ，以驗證管理成效。

Energy and Resources Management
The Group has always regarded the protection of the environment and natural 
resources as a key priority for the sustainable development of the Company.  
We promote green transition through multiple paths, striving to achieve  
a synergistic enhancement of resource benefits and ecological benefits.  
Upholding the principle of source reduction, we systematically reduce resource  
and energy consumption during operations, and continuously optimize  
utilization efficiency. While ensuring our stable operations, we persistently  
reduce consumption per unit of output, mitigate negative impacts on the  
environment, further improve overall operating efficiency and environmental  
performance, t hereby comprehensively stimulating new momentum for green 
development.
Energy Management
The energy consumed by the Group during the Reporting Period was 
comprised of gasoline, diesel oil, natural gas, electricity, and liquefied  
petroleum gas. Nanjing Sinolife places great emphasis on improving energy  
use efficiency, regarding it as a core measure in implementing its low-carbon  
development strategy. We actively implement energy-saving measures  
throughout the entire production and operation process to maximize the  
reduction of energy consumption, thereby reducing environmental impact  
and improving overall operational efficiency.
In t erms of compliance management, in consideration of the actual operating 
conditions of each base, the Group formulates corresponding standard  
operating procedures (SOPs) for energy management facilities in strict  
compliance with the relevant laws and regulations of the places where  
we operate, such as the Law of the People ’s Republic of China on Energy  
Conservation, and the New Zealand Resource Management Act 1991, to  
ensure t hat energy-saving and emission-reduction measures are implemented 
systematically and efficiently.
To continuously advance energy efficiency upgrades, we systematically
  
establish a n energy management system, deepen energy-saving optimization 
of production processes, and perfect the dynamic monitoring mechanism  
for energy efficiency, thereby comprehensively promoting energy saving and  
efficiency improvement across the value chain. Meanwhile, by integrating  
innovative c oncepts throughout the entire process of R&D design, production 
technology, and daily operations, we continue to promote technological
  
upgrades a nd process improvements, as part of our commitments to avoiding 
unnecessary energy consumption from the source, thereby achieving more
  
efficient resource utilization.
能源與資源管理
本集團始終將環境與天然資源的保護視為企
業可持續發展的關鍵命題
 ，通過多元路徑推
動綠色轉型 ，致力實現資源效益與生態效益
的 協 同 提 升 。我 們 秉 持 源 頭 減 量 的 原 則，系
統性降低營運過程中的資源與能源耗用 ，並
持續優化使用效率 ，在保障穩定運營的同時，
持續降低單位產出消耗 ，減少對環境的負面
影響 ，進一步提升整體經營效率與環境績效，
全方位激發綠色發展新動能
 。
能源管理

能耗 ，以減少環境影響並提升整體經營效率。

Environmental, Social and Governance Report
環境、社會及管治報告
Water Resource Management
Effective water resource management is not only a guarantee for the Company’s 
stable operations, but also an important cornerstone for maintaining  
ecological and environmental sustainability. The Group fully recognizes the  
importance of water resource management, and therefore strictly complies  
with the water resource management regulations of the places where we  
operate, including the Water Law of People ’s Republic of China  and the New 
Zealand Water Services Act, 2021 . On this basis, all of our subsidiaries have 
formulated a nd implemented rigorous water management standard operating 
procedures tailored to their respective business characteristics and actual water  
consumption, whereby the entire process of water intake, usage, and discharge  
during p roduction and operations is subject to the standardized management 
to ensure the safe and compliant use of water resources.
The water used by the Group is mainly sourced from municipal water supply  
and rainwater reuse, and therefore there are no issues such as difficulty in  
sourcing water or insufficient water supply. The water supply has always  
been stable and controllable, effectively supporting the orderly development  
of the Group ’s various business activities. Water consumption is mainly  
concentrated in the daily operation of the office premises and production  
in the industry park. To comprehensively enhance water efficiency, we  
actively promote refined water resource management strategies, including  
introducing a  series of initiatives to construct a water-saving operating system. 
By continuously utilizing various monitoring platforms, we track the water  
usage dynamics in real time. Owing to the renovation of old pipe networks and 
the widespread application of water-saving appliances, we effectively reduce  
water t ransmission losses and terminal waste. At the same time, we proactively 
promote source reduction and recycling, by introducing rainwater recycling  
systems to gradually realize diversified replenishment and recycling of water  
resources, reducing our reliance on the municipal water supply.
水資源管理
良好的水資源管理不僅是企業穩定運營的保
障，更是維護生態環境可持續性的重要基石
 。
本集團深知水資源管理的重要意義 ，嚴格遵
循 運 營 所 在 地 的 水 資 源 管 理 法 規 ，包 括 《中
華人民共和國水法 》 及紐西蘭的 《水務法案，
2021》 等 。在此基礎上，各子公司結合自身業
務特點與用水實際情況
 ，均制定並實施了嚴
謹的用水管理標準作業程序 ，對生產運營中
的取水 、用水、排水全過程進行規範化管理，
確保水資源使用安全合規
 。
本集團的用水主要來源於市政用水與雨水回
用，不 存 在 任 何 水 源 求 取 困 難
 、用 水 供 應 不
足 等 問 題 ，用 水 供 應 長 期 處 於 穩 定、可 控 狀
態，有 效 支 撐 集 團 各 項 經 營 活 動 有 序 開 展
 。
水資源消耗主要集中在園區日常運營辦公與
生 產 。為 全 面 提 升 用 水 效 率，我 們 積 極 推 行
水資源精細化管理策略 ，通過一系列舉措建
構節水型營運體系 。我們通過持續監測平台
即時掌握用水動態 ，結合老舊管網改造與節
水器具的廣泛應用 ，有效降低輸水損耗與終
端 浪 費 。同 時，我 們 積 極 推 動 源 頭 減 量 與 循
環 再 用
，引 入 雨 水 回 收 系 統，逐 步 實 現 水 資
源的多元補給與循環利用
 ，降低對市政供水
的依賴 。

mitigate risks of water accumulation 
while enhancing the standardized

101南京中生聯合股份有限公司  二零二五年年報
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
環境、社會及管治報告
Environment and Natural Resources
The Group attaches great importance to environmental protection, thereby 
considering it as an important cornerstone of our corporate sustainable  
development. During the production and operation process, we always  
strive to minimize our impact on the environment and natural resources.  
We strictly implement various environmental policies and regulations in all  
aspects of production and operations. By introducing various measures such  
as optimizing production processes, standardizing production practices, and  
selecting eco-friendly raw materials, we actively advance our environmental  
protection campaign such as waste recycling and innovative raw material  
substitution, effectively avoiding and reducing the generation of various  
pollutants, and continuously reducing the negative impact on the ecological  
environment. R egarding natural raw materials such as honey and herbal crops 
required for production, we provide clean operational equipment, strictly  
restrict on-site personnel access, standardize on-site hygiene management,  
and establish a comprehensive regular inspection and monitoring system. In  
addition to specifying isolation procedures and safe disposal standards, we  
regularly a ppoint pest control experts to conduct on-site inspections, thereby 
comprehensively g
 uaranteeing the environmental compliance of the sourcing 
and processing of natural raw materials. The above environmental protection  
measures not only effectively protect natural resources and safeguard the  
balance of the ecological environment, but also help reduce interference to  
the ecosystems, earnestly fulfilling the green development philosophy and  
building a  solid foundation for the sustainable development of the ecological 
environment.
During the Reporting Period, the Group strictly complied with environmental
  
regulatory requirements. We were not subject to any administrative penalties  
related t o environmental protection, nor were any incidents with a significant 
impact on the environment and natural resources identified in the places  
where we operate, thereby earnestly fulfilling our primary responsibility for  
environmental protection.

 ，在生產運營過程

多 項 舉 措 ，積 極 推 進 廢 料 回 收 利 用、創 新 原

 ，有效避免並減少各類污

NANJING SINOLIFE UNITED COMPANY LIMITED  ANNU AL REPORT 2025102
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
環境、社會及管治報告
Response to Climate Change
Nanjing Sinolife is fully conscious of the profound impact of climate change 
on business and social development, and we have taken a series of actions  
to respond to this global challenge. In active response to the national “Dual  
Carbon ” strategy, we reduce greenhouse gas emissions in the operating  
processes o f the Company and its value chain, and enhance our own resilience 
against c
limate change risks by implementing the climate risk and opportunity 
management, energy conservation and consumption reduction measures.
With reference to the “Climate-related Disclosures ” under Part D of the ESG
  
Code of the Stock Exchange and IFRS S2 “Climate-related Disclosures” issued by  
the I nternational Sustainability Standards Board (ISSB), we continuously identify 
and analyze the climate change factors impacting the Group ’s operations,  
and f ormulate a series of strategies and measures to address climate risks and 
opportunities based on our actual conditions, actively responding to climate
  
change.
Governance
The Group places great importance on climate change governance and 
continuously optimizes internal climate change management mechanisms. 
We have established a three-tier ESG governance structure, including the 
Board, the ESG Management Committee, and the ESG Task Force, to manage 
ESG issues including climate change. As the highest decision-making body 
of the Group, the Board primarily assumes the core responsibilities of setting 
carbon reduction targets and formulating the overall strategy for responding 
to climate change, providing directions and building a solid foundation for 
the Group ’s climate governance. The ESG Management Committee focuses 
on specific implementation supervision, regularly reviewing various climate 
change-related matters, monitoring the progress of the carbon reduction 
target implementation, and periodically reviewing and dynamically updating 
the identified climate risk lists to ensure that climate management strategies 
are scientific, feasible, and effective. As the specific executing unit, the ESG 
Task Force is primarily responsible for promoting the implementation of the 
climate risk response strategy and coordinating various departments to carry out 
relevant work, ensuring that all stages of work proceed in an orderly and efficient 
manner. The ESG Task Force reports work progress to its superiors on a regular 
basis, detailing the completion of carbon reduction targets, the results of climate 
risk and opportunity identification, and the progress of various tasks, forming 
a closed-loop management mechanism to ensure the Group stays informed 
of w
ork dynamics and accurately deploys subsequent tasks. Meanwhile, upon 
receiving the ESG report for the year, relevant departments of the Company 
regularly hold special meetings to comprehensively review the implementation 
of ESG initiatives and the extent to which targets have been met. These meetings 
focus on in-depth discussions and assessments regarding the ESG-related work 
planning and specific implementation arrangements for the next year, as well 
as the improvement paths and implementation measures for various key ESG 
indicators, fully listening to opinions and suggestions from all parties to ensure 
that the ESG work deployment for the coming year is scientifically sound, 
reasonable and practicable. This approach continuously drives improvements 
in the Company ’s ESG management level and information disclosure quality, 
thereby better addressing the concerns of stakeholders.
應對氣候變化
南京中生充分認識到氣候變化對業務和社會
發展的深遠影響
 ，我們已經採取了一系列行
動來應對這一全球性挑戰 。我們積極響應國
家 「 雙 碳」 戰 略，通 過 氣 候 風 險 機 遇 管 理、節
能降耗等方式
 ，減少本公司及其價值鏈運營
過程中的溫室氣體排放 ，提升自身對氣候變
化風險的韌性 。
我 們 參 考 香 港 聯 交 所 《 ESG 守 則》D 部 分 「氣 候
相關披露 」 準則及國際可持續準則理事會 (ISSB)
發布的 《 國際財務報告可持續披露準則第 2 號 
— 氣 候 相 關 披 露 》(IFRS S2) 持 續 識 別 並 分 析 對
集團運營產生影響的氣候變化因素 ，並結合
實際情況制定一系列應對氣候風險和機遇的
策略和措施 ，積極應對氣候變化。
管治
本集團高度重視氣候變化管治工作，持續優
化內部的氣候變化管理機制 。我們已成立包
括董事會 、ESG 管理委員會及 ESG 工作小組的
三 級ESG 管 治 架 構，對 包 括 氣 候 變 化 在 內 的
ESG 議題進行管理 。作為集團最高決策機構，
董事會主要承擔減碳目標制定 、應對氣候變
化整體戰略規劃的核心職責 ，為集團開展氣
候治理工作指明方向 、築牢基礎。ESG 管理委
員會則聚焦具體執行監督 ，定期對與氣候變
化相關的各項工作進行審閱 ，跟蹤減碳目標
的落地進度 ，同時對已識別的氣候風險清單
開展定期審議與動態更新 ，保障氣候管理策
略科學可行 、落地見效。ESG 工作小組作為具
體執行單元 ，核心負責推動氣候風險應對戰
略的落地實施 ，統籌協調各部門開展相關工
作，確 保 各 環 節 工 作 有 序 推 進 、高 效 落 地。
ESG 工 作 小 組 會 定 期 向 上 級 層 級 匯 報 工 作 進
度，詳 細 說 明 減 碳 目 標 完 成 情 況 、氣 候 風 險
與機遇識別結果及各項工作推進情況 ，形成
閉 環 管 理 ，確 保 集 團 及 時 掌 握 工 作 動 態、精
準部署後續工作
 。同時，在取得當年 ESG 報告
後，公 司 相 關 部 門 定 期 召 開 專 題 會 議 ，全 面
梳理 E SG 工作的執行情況、目標達成度，重點
圍繞下一年度 E SG 相關工作規劃、具體實施安
排，以及 E SG 各項核心指標的提升路徑、實施
舉措開展深入討論與評估 ，充分聽取各方意
見 建 議，確 保 下 一 年 度ESG 工 作 部 署 科 學 合
理、切實可行 ，持續推動公司 ESG 管理水平與
信息披露質量提升 ，更好地回應利益相關方
的關切 。

Through the above-mentioned “decision-making —  management — 
execution” three-tier interactive system, we ensure that climate-related issues  
can be integrated into our day-to-day operations and systematically brought  
under control. For details of the governance structure, please refer to the  
abovementioned section headed “ESG Governance Structure”.
We assist the Board and the management in keeping abreast of the latest  
guidelines, policy developments and corresponding response measures  
related to climate change through regular review of ESG-related reports and  
continuous c ommunication with external consultants, so as to strengthen their 
expertise and competence in climate-related issues. Currently, the Group has  
not incorporated climate-related factors into its remuneration policies.
Strategy
The Group has identified and managed climate-related risks and opportunities, 
and formulated relevant preventive measures to reduce the negative impact  
of climate risks on the Group. Based on the Company ’s business types and  
operations, we have identified physical risks and transition risks with greater  
impact and probability, and explored potential opportunities. We recognize  
that these climate risks may have a material impact on the Company in the  
short term (within 3 years), medium term (3–5 years), and long term (over  
5 years), which covers the physical changes of the environment and the  
challenges brought by the transition to a low-carbon economy.
To effectively respond to these risks, the Group analyzes their impact on our risk  
control management objectives and evaluates the Company ’s risk tolerance.  
Based on the identification results, we have formulated corresponding  
response measures for physical risks (such as extreme weather, natural  
disasters as a result of climate change) and transition risks (such as policy  
changes, technological upgrades, and market demand changes), ensuring  
that the Company maintains a proactive and forward-looking stance in risk  
management.
通過上述 「 決策 — 管理  — 執行 」 三級聯動的
縱深體系 ，我們確保氣候相關議題能夠融入
日 常 運 營 ，並 得 到 系 統 性 管 控。有 關 管 治 架
構詳情
，請見前文 「ESG 管治架構」 小節。

候變化相關的最新指引 、政策動向及相應的
應對措施 ，從而強化其在氣候相關議題上的
專 業 知 識 與 勝 任 能 力
 。目 前，本 集 團 尚 未 將
氣候相關因素納入薪酬政策 。
策略
本集團已針對與氣候相關風險和機遇進行了
識 別 及 管 理
 ，同 時 制 定 了 相 關 防 範 措 施，減
少氣候風險為集團帶來的負面影響 。從公司
業務類型與營運角度出發 ，我們識別出了具
有較大影響和可能性的實體風險與轉型風險 ，
並探索了潛在的機遇 。我們認識到這些氣候
風險可能在短期 （3 年以內 ） 、中期 （3–5 年） 和
長期 （ 5 年以上） 對本公司產生重要影響，其中
涵蓋環境的物理變化以及向低碳經濟轉型帶
來的挑戰 。
為了有效應對這些風險 ，本集團分析其對風
控管理目標的影響程度 ，並評估公司的風險
承 受 能 力 。根 據 識 別 結 果，我 們 針 對 實 體 風
險 （ 如極端天氣、氣候變化引發的自然災害）
和轉型風險 （
 如政策變化、技術升級和市場需
求變化 ） 制定了相應的應對措施，確保企業在
風險管理中保持主動性與前瞻性
 。

Risk Management
The Group has established a comprehensive risk management strategy 
covering the entire process of climate change risk identification, assessment  
and management. To enhance management efficiency and optimize  
resource allocation, we have comprehensively integrated climate change risk  
management i nto the Group’s o verall risk management system, systematically 
assessed the identified relevant risks, and formulated targeted control measures  
to ensure that all types of climate change risks remain within a controllable  
range, gradually improving the Company’s resilience against climate change.
During the year, we continued the established risk identification standards  
and processes to carry out comprehensive risk investigation. Following the  
systematic assessment, there were no material changes in the risk identification  
results for the year, and all types of potential risks were within the expected  
controllable range.

Indicators and Targets
Combating climate change has become a global consensus. Based on the 
identification of climate risks and opportunities, the Board of the Group has  
set c limate targets and monitors the progress of greenhouse gas emission and 
energy consumption reduction efforts. These initiatives drive our transition
  
toward a lower-carbon and more sustainable business model.
To systematically promote our environmental protection, optimize  
management p aths and continuously enhance environmental performance in 
all l inks, the Group has formulated clear environmental targets and indicators. 
In 2021, we set five-year (2022 to 2026) environmental targets for key areas  
such as greenhouse gas emissions, energy consumption, use of water resource,  
and waste emissions.

環境、社會及管治報告
During the Reporting Period, the Group effectively reduced total 
greenhouse gas emissions and various resource consumption through  
regular environmental assessments and the implementation of targeted  
improvement measures, thereby laying a solid foundation for the steady  
advancement of long-term carbon reduction targets and green sustainable  
development strategies. During the year, the Group ’s Scope I and Scope II  
greenhouse gas emissions decreased by 8.6% compared to last year, the  
overall energy consumption decreased by 1.6%, and the packaging material  
consumption d ecreased by 4.4%. Although the water consumption and waste 
emissions increased due to the expansion of the Group ’s business scale and  
the continuous growth in the number of employees, they remained within  
a controllable range. Subsequently, the Group will continue to optimize  
environmental control measures and strengthen green operation management  
to fully promote the continuous optimization and steady improvement of  
various environmental indicators.
In t erms of climate-related management, the Group invested RMB1,320,000 in 
the R&D field in 2025, which was used for product R&D and core technology  
upgrades. By increasing investment in technological R&D, optimizing product  
R&D processes and upgrading existing technology systems, the Company  
continues to enhance its R&D capabilities and conversion efficiency for new  
product technologies, while strengthening its ability to respond quickly to  
market dynamics and customer needs to better cope with technical risks  
and market risks, laying a foundation for the long-term stable development  
of the Company. The Group invested RMB141,000 in safety production,  
mainly used for various expenses such as safety protection devices, safety  
equipment and fire drills, to further enhance the risk prevention and control  
awareness and response capabilities of all employees, effectively mitigate  
various r isks including those brought about by climate change, and assist the 
Company in gradually improving its climate risk management system. These
  
investments are not only the concrete implementation of our climate-related  
management work, but also important measures for the Group to practice  
the concept of green sustainable development and strengthen climate risk  
prevention a nd control. Furthermore, they serve as core support for advancing 
the achievement of carbon reduction targets and improving environmental  
management levels, thereby fully demonstrating the Group ’s high regard for  
climate-related m anagement and its strong sense of social responsibility, while 
building a  foundation and providing a guarantee for the further advancement 
of subsequent climate management tasks. During the Reporting Period, the
  
Group had not yet established an internal carbon pricing mechanism. For other  
cross-industry metrics, we implemented the Reasonable Information Relief, and  
do not disclose the amounts and percentages of assets or business activities  
vulnerable to climate-related risks and opportunities for the time being.

raw material procurement to finished product ex-factory under a systematic  
quality control system. Driven by consumer needs, we continue to advance  
product innovation and quality upgrades to ensure that all products meet  
the highest standards. Guided by our “Quality First ” philosophy, which is  
embedded in every aspect of our operations, we are committed to providing  
consumers with safe and reliable product experiences.

complaints regarding serious violations of product quality and safety-related

requirements o f the places where we operate, we ensure a solid foundation for 
compliant operations.

Quality Management System
The Group strictly complies with relevant product quality, food safety and 
pharmaceutical production laws and regulations in the places where we  
operate, including but not limited to the Food Safety Law of the People ’s 
Republic of China  and its implementing regulations, the Food Safety Risk 
Control Checklist , the Regulations on Supervision and Administration of the 
Implementation of the Main Responsibility for Food Safety by Enterprises , 
the Measures for the Administration of Supervision and Inspection of Food 
Production and Operation , as well as the New Zealand Dietary Supplements 
Regulations 1985 , the New Zealand Animal Products Act 1999 , the New 
Zealand Food Act 2014  and the Good Manufacturing Practice of Medical 
Products (GMP) , etc. We have developed internal management systems 
such as the Food Safety Management System and the Food Safety Risk 
Management System for Daily Control , Weekly Investigation and Monthly 
Adjustment , comprehensively integrating compliance requirements into the 
entire production and operation process to ensure that every stage of the  
product from raw materials to finished products meets regulatory standards,  
guaranteeing product safety and reliability.
At t he same time, we closely track the updates of laws and regulations related 
to product quality, food safety and pharmaceutical production in the places  
where we operate, thereby ensuring that compliance management always  
remains proactive and effective. During the Reporting Period, we systematically 
drafted and revised a total of 25 internal record documents in accordance  
with the latest regulatory requirements. These documents help ensure that  
all operational activities consistently comply with the provisions of applicable  
laws and regulations.
We a lways regard product quality and safety management as a core priority of 
corporate operations. Through institutional development and organizational  
structure optimisation, we ensure the transparency and controllability of the  
entire process from raw materials to finished products. We have formulated  
the Code of Responsibilities for Food Safety Director and Food Safety Officer , 
systematically d efining the responsibilities and behavioral norms of personnel 
at all levels in quality management, thereby providing solid institutional  
safeguards for product safety work. In terms of organizational structure, we  
established a scientifically sound internal quality and safety management  
system t o ensure efficient communication and rapid response to quality issues 
across different management levels. In addition, we assembled a professional
  
quality team and formally appointed the responsible person, food safety  
director, and food safety officers in accordance with relevant procedures,  
ensuring c lear accountability and well-defined responsibilities, with the quality 
responsibility being precisely assigned to the specific positions and individuals.

 ，為產品安全工作提供堅實的制

To ensure the effectiveness of and continuous improvement in quality 
management, the Group sets clear annual quality targets each year. During  
the Reporting Period, we set “zero major food safety and quality incidents  
throughout the year and compliant sales processes ” as our core quality  
management target. During the Reporting Period, this target was fully  
achieved, w ith no major food safety and quality incidents recorded throughout 
the year and no regulatory violations in the production and sales processes,
  
thereby demonstrating the effective operation of the Group ’s quality  
management system and the proper functioning of its control measures.
To comprehensively enhance the quality awareness and professional  
capabilities of all employees, we organize and conduct regular quality  
management-related training sessions. In 2025, the Group completed a  
total of 9 internal training sessions related to food safety and quality in strict  
accordance with the annual training plan, and participated in 2 external  
thematic training sessions on regulations and standards. Such completion  
effectively enhanced the team ’s understanding and execution capabilities  
of the latest regulatory requirements. Through continuous training  
empowerment, we further consolidated the organizational foundation of  
quality management, which ensured that various institutional norms are  
effectively implemented in operations.
During the Reporting Period, the Group ’s brands Living Nature and Good
  
Health continued to maintain several international authoritative certifications,  
showcasing the excellent standard of product quality and safety management.

Quality Control
The Group always prioritizes product quality and safety as the primary factors 
of our business development, continuously improving quality and safety  
management s ystems to actively meet evolving market demands and mitigate 
potential r isks. We strengthened full-chain closed-loop management from raw 
material procurement, production and manufacturing, product packaging to
  
logistics and transportation, ensuring every link complies with international  
standards and regulatory requirements in the target market, providing  
consumers with safe and reliable product safeguards.
Sinolife United implements full-process coverage and multi-dimensional  
refined control over product quality, which not only extends quality control  
to every segment including raw material procurement, production and  
manufacturing, n ew product inspection, finished product ex-factory and after-
sales supervision, but also builds a comprehensive quality assurance network  
from institutional construction, supplier review, arrival inspection, process  
monitoring to continuous improvement.
During the Reporting Period, Sinolife United engaged third-party testing
  
agencies to conduct 11 batches of type inspections throughout the year, all  
of which met compliance standards, ensuring product quality aligns with  
standard requirements.

123南京中生聯合股份有限公司  二零二五年年報

環境、社會及管治報告
Through the Living Nature Quality System Manual, Living Nature has 
established a full-process quality control system from raw material receipt  
to product delivery, and continues to enhance and optimize the quality  
management system. In the production process, rigorous inspection  
procedures a re implemented for each stage to ensure that products meet high 
standards. To prevent issues such as coding errors or unclear expiration date  
labels, Living Nature implements a dual inspection mechanism, under which  
at l east two quality inspectors are required to conduct a comprehensive check 
on p
roduct quality, safety, and coding information and sign off before the next 
production stage. To ensure the effective operation of the quality management
  
system, L iving Nature regularly conducts systematic internal reviews of quality 
control p
rocesses and batch production records every year. At the same time, 
the Company has established a long-term cooperation relationship with
  
third-party testing institutions with professional qualifications to conduct  
comprehensive quality testing on products every year, thereby ensuring their  
continuous compliance with high standards.
Good Health has established a quality control system throughout the entire  
production process, under which strict process control is implemented over  
product production to ensure that every stage meets high-standard quality  
requirements. The quality control department is responsible for reviewing  
the quality results, and comprehensively auditing various monitoring  
documents a nd records during the production process to ensure data integrity 
and compliance. In terms of the supply chain management, Good Health  
requires all suppliers to strictly adhere to GMP requirements by executing  
comprehensive and meticulous Raw Material Assessments (RMA) targeting  
new product development and the quality team and by conducting rigorous  
sample testing and trial production procedures. Furthermore, multiple  
inspection procedures are established in the manufacturing and packaging  
segments to further strengthen quality assurance as part of our commitment  
to providing consumers with safe and reliable health products.
Living Nature 通 過 《Living Nature 質 量 體 系 手
冊》 ， 建立了從原材料入庫到產品出廠的全流
程質量控制體系 ，並持續推動品質管理系統
的 改 善 與 優 化 。在 生 產 過 程 中，每 一 個 環 節
均設有嚴格的檢驗程序 ，以確保產品符合高
標準要求 。為避免編碼錯誤或有效期限標示
不清等問題
 ，Living Nature 實施雙重質檢機制，
要求至少兩名質檢員對產品質量 、安全性及
編碼資訊進行全面檢查並簽名確認後 ，方可
進入下一道生產工序 。為確保品質管理體系
有效運行 ，Living Nature 每年定期對品質控制
流程及批量生產記錄進行系統性內部審查 。

構建立長期合作關係
 ，每年對產品進行全面

好健康建立了貫穿整個生產過程的質量管控

125南京中生聯合股份有限公司  二零二五年年報

環境、社會及管治報告
Product Recall
In strict compliance with relevant laws and regulations in the places where 
we operate, including the Food Safety Law of the People ’s Republic of China  
and the Food Product Recall Management Rules , the Group formulates a 
collection of internal policy documents, including the User Complaints and 
Adverse Reactions Management Procedure , the Products Recall Management 
Procedure  and the Incoming Food Inspection Procedures , to ensure a 
standardized response to potential product health and safety incidents.
We always place the rights and interests of customers and consumers at the  
core of our operations, and have developed and continuously improved  
product recall mechanisms tailored to various market needs. We continue  
to strengthen the applicability and resilience of recall mechanisms in various  
markets by requiring annual product recall drills at all operational sites on a  
regular basis to validate process feasibility and emergency response speed.  
This enables rapid and accurate recall actions when necessary, thereby  
guaranteeing consumer safety and brand trust. In 2025, in strict compliance  
with the annual work plan and relevant compliance requirements, Sinolife  
United successfully launched and completed the dedicated product recall  
drill. This drill had comprehensively tested the integrity of the Company ’s 
recall system, emergency response capability, and the cross-departmental  
collaboration efficiency, which in return effectively enhanced the emergency  
handling literacy and practical operational ability of personnel in relevant  
positions, and laid a solid foundation for the Company to subsequently and  
efficiently address various product recall scenarios, protect consumer rights  
and interests, and strengthen the foundation of compliant operations.
During t
he year, Living Nature further upgraded the Corrective and Preventive 
Actions ( CAPA) process, which is aimed at standardizing the investigation and 
handling procedures for defects and out-of-specification results to ensure
  
that t he root causes are identified and effective measures are taken to prevent 
recurrence. When an incident occurs, the respective department manager
  
conducts a n investigation, prepares a CAPA report, and simultaneously reports 
the i
ssue, investigation findings, and control measures to the quality manager 
and the senior management team. Where the defect exists in newly received
  
materials, the reasons for non-compliance are analyzed through the CAPA. In  
case that the defective product has been released, the quality manager and the  
department manager collaboratively investigate and specify relevant measures,  
and jointly evaluate with the senior management team whether to initiate  
the recall procedures, as well as the type and urgency of the recall. Through  
processes such as incident registration and identification, investigation and  
evaluation, correction and prevention, and review and closure, Living Nature  
has achieved closed-loop management, thereby further strengthening the  
integrity and reliability of the quality system.

本集團嚴格遵守運營所在地相關法律法規，
包 括 《

 》 等，並制定 《用戶投訴及不良

貨查驗制度 》 等內部制度，確保能夠規範應對
潛在的產品健康安全事件 。
我們始終將客戶與消費者的權益置於核心地
位，建立並持續完善適配各市場需求的產品

 ，要求各營運點每年定期

2025 年

礎奠定了堅實基礎
 。
本 年 度，Living Nature 進 一 步 升 級 了 糾 正 與

 。若缺

關 閉 等 流 程
 ，Living Nature 實 現 了 閉 環 管 理，

127南京中生聯合股份有限公司  二零二五年年報

Innovation Research
The Group regards research and development innovation as the core driving 
force leading development. By closely aligning with market orientation,  
and keenly gaining insights into consumer preferences and industry trends,  
the Group steadily launches high-quality and high-value-added products  
through continuous technological innovation and product upgrades. To  
ensure t he compliance of the R&D process, the Group systematically manages 
processes such as the approval, research, project approval, and review of
  
product R&D requirements through internal systems, in strict compliance  
with relevant product R&D laws and regulations in the places where we  
operate, including the Regulations of the People ’s Republic of China on the  
Registration and Administration of Overseas Manufacturers of Imported Food  
(General Administration of Customs Order No. 248) , the Measures of the 
People’s Republic of China for the Administration of Import and Export Food  
Safety , as well as the Measures on the Administration of Cosmetic Labelling 
(National Medical Products Administration Order No. 77), the Measures for 
the Administration of the Registration and Recordation of Cosmetics (State  
Administration for Market Regulation Order No. 35) , the Regulations on the 
Administration of Supplementary Inspection Methods for Cosmetics, the  
Specification for the Evaluation of the Claimed Efficacy of Cosmetics , the 
Regulations for the Administration of Cosmetics Registration and Filing Data 
and the Technical Guidelines for the Safety Assessment of Cosmetics.
The Group closely integrates product innovation with the growing demand  
for nutritional well-being among consumers, adheres to the guidance of the  
European Clean Label management concept, and strives to reduce or eliminate  
the u se of additives in new product design. In terms of raw material selection, 
we prioritize high-quality raw materials such as naturally sourced A2 milk
  
protein and microbial fermentation-derived ingredients, thereby committing  
ourselves to providing consumers with safe, healthy and high-quality products.
We attach great importance to the R&D investment. In 2025, we invested a  
cumulative R &D funding of RMB1.32 million and successfully implemented 35 
technology i nnovation projects, continuing to leverage R&D innovation as the 
core driving force to help the enterprise achieve high-quality and sustainable  
development.
During the Reporting Period, the Group launched a total of 27 new products,
  
including 2 3 Good Health series products and 4 Living Nature series products, 
continuously e nriching the product matrix to respond to the diversified market 
demands.

本集團將研發創新視為引領發展的核心動力，
緊扣市場導向
 ，敏銳洞察消費者偏好與行業
走 向 ，藉 由 持 續 的 技 術 革 新 與 產 品 升 級，實
現 高 品 質
、高 附 加 價 值 產 品 的 穩 步 推 出。為
確保研發過程合規
 ，本集團嚴格遵循運營地

《中華人民共和國進口食品境外生產企業註冊

Intellectual Property Protection
The Group has always regarded the protection of intellectual property rights 
and patent inventions as an important factor in innovative development.  
During the Reporting Period, the Group did not experience any incidents of  
intellectual p roperty infringement, showcasing our effectiveness in compliance 
management and risk prevention.
At the institutional development level, we strictly comply with relevant laws  
and r egulations in the places where we operate, such as the Patent Law of the 
People’s Republic of China , and formulated the Intellectual Property Rights 
Management a nd Patent Protection System , which systematically standardizes 
key p rocesses such as patent application, daily maintenance and infringement 
response. This ensures that every innovation achievement receives effective  
institutional safeguards.
In terms of personnel training and awareness enhancement, Sinolife United  
adopts diverse initiatives to enhance employees ’ capabilities in intellectual  
property protection. We not only regularly invite professional trademark  
agencies to conduct specialized seminars, providing in-depth analysis of  
key points of trademark law and practical case studies, but also organize  
systematic t raining for the research and development team on patent law and 
relevant policies and regulations, ensuring team members comprehensive  
understanding o f the critical knowledge from application to rights protection. 
Through continuous training and promotional activities, we effectively  
enhance employees ’ awareness of intellectual property protection, building  
a s olid line of defense for the Company ’s t echnological innovation and brand 
value.
During t he Reporting Period, we continued to maintain the validity of existing 
invention patents, and in alignment with the Company ’s international  
development strategy, we carried out trademark registration applications in  
multiple Southeast Asian countries to strengthen our overseas intellectual  
property portfolio. During the year, we carried out trademark expiration  
renewals for a total of 17 cases, of which 11 have been successfully renewed.  
Among newly applied trademarks, 6 cases have been approved (including 4 for  
Good H ealth and 2 for Living Nature), and another 10 Good Health trademarks 
are currently in the application stage. As of the end of the Reporting Period, the
  
Group held a total of 278 trademarks, including 94 under Sinolife United, 112  
under Good Health, and 72 under Living Nature.

Privacy Protection and Information Security

Group in safeguarding consumer rights and interests. In strict compliance

Responsible Supply Chain
To build an efficient and stable supply chain system, the Group continuously 
deepens upstream and downstream collaboration and cooperation, and  
ensures the smooth operation of all processes through the optimized  
allocation of resources. At the same time, upholding the concept of win-win  
cooperation, we continue to improve supply chain management initiatives,  
and c ommit ourselves to driving the industry toward a more environmentally 
friendly and efficient future, thereby creating long-term value for society.
Management System and Policies
The Group’s supply chain network covers multiple geographical regions, with  
its major suppliers concentrated in New Zealand, and others simultaneously  
located i n countries such as Australia, France, Germany, and China. Its supplier 
base comprises raw material manufacturers, finished product processing
  
plants, packaging material manufacturers, and logistics service providers,  
thereby f orming a complete and collaborative industry chain system. Procured 
raw materials include plant extracts, animal extracts, packaging materials, and
  
finished products, meeting diversified product demands.
We are always committed to building a sustainable and responsible supply  
chain. From strict control at the source of raw material procurement to  
seamless collaboration with logistics partners, distributors, and retailers, we  
are committed to providing consumers with safe and high-quality health  
products. By strictly selecting suppliers who demonstrate a high willingness  
to cooperate, superior service capabilities, and excellent product quality, we  
advance the synergistic development of the value chain to achieve mutual  
benefits and win-win results. For overseas suppliers, we adopt a collaborative  
management model at home and abroad, and conduct comprehensive  
reviews in strict compliance with GMP standards to ensure the stability and  
compliance o f production and operations, thereby providing a solid safeguard 
for the sustainable development of the supply chain.
We have established a comprehensive supply chain management system,
  
and formulate and continuously optimize internal policies, such as the  
Supplier Management System , to ensure the efficient operation of all stages 
of the supply chain. During the Reporting Period, in respect of the specific  
supplier category of “overseas food production ” characterized by high risk  
and high compliance requirements, we updated and refined internal policy  
documents, including the Overseas Food Production Enterprise Audit System  
and the Overseas Food Production Enterprise Audit Record . Under the new 
policy framework, stricter and more operational exclusive audit procedures  
are formulated to focus on the review and conclusive determination of  
the systematic compliance and risk control capabilities of food production  
enterprises, r ather than basic information collection. This further improved the 
Group’s supplier management system and strengthened the control over the  
distribution of its global supply network.
負責任供應鏈
為構建高效穩定的供應鏈體系，本集團持續
深化上下游協同合作 ，通過資源的優化配置，
確 保 各 環 節 順 暢 運 作 。同 時，我 們 秉 持 合 作
共 贏 的 理 念 ，不 斷 改 進 供 應 鏈 管 理 舉 措，致
力 於 推 動 行 業 邁 向 更 環 保 、高 效 的 未 來，為
社會創造長期價值 。
管理體系與制度
本集團供應鏈網絡覆蓋多個地理區域，主要
供 應 商 集 中 於 紐 西 蘭
 ，同 時 分 佈 於 澳 洲、法
國、德 國 、中 國 等 國 家。供 應 商 類 型 涵 蓋 原
料 生 產 廠
、成 品 加 工 廠、包 裝 材 料 生 產 廠 及
物 流 服 務 商
 ，形 成 完 整 的 產 業 鏈 協 作 體 系。
採 購 原 材 料 包 括 植 物 提 取 物 、動 物 提 取 物、
包裝材料及成品等 ，滿足多元化的產品需求。
我們始終致力於建構可持續和負責任的供應
鏈，從 原 物 料 採 購 的 源 頭 嚴 格 把 關 ，到 與 物
流、分 銷 及 零 售 商 的 緊 密 協 作 ，致 力 於 為 消
費 者 提 供 安 全 、優 質 的 健 康 產 品。我 們 嚴 格
篩選具備高度合作意願 、優質服務能力及卓
越產品質量的供應商 ，推動價值鏈的協同發
展，實 現 互 利 共 贏 。針 對 海 外 供 應 商，我 們
採 用 國 內 外 協 同 管 理 模 式 ，嚴 格 參 照GMP 標
準進行全面審核 ，確保生產運營的穩定性與
合規性，為供應鏈的可持續發展提供堅實保障。
我們建立健全供應鏈管理體系 ，通過制訂並
持續優化 《 供應商管理制度》 等內部政策，確
保 供 應 鏈 各 環 節 的 高 效 運 作 。報 告 期 內，我
們針對 「 境外食品生產」 這一高風險、高合規
要求的特殊供應商類別 ，更新並細化了內部
制度文件 ，包括 《境外食品生產企業審核制度》
及 《
境外食品生產企業審核記錄》 。新制度制
定 更 嚴 格 、更 具 操 作 性 的 專 屬 審 核 規 程，著
重於對食品生產企業系統性合規與風險控制
能力的評審與結論判定 ，而非僅限於基礎資
訊收集 ，進一步完善了集團的供應商管理體
系，加強了對全球化供應網絡佈局的管控 。

Supplier Management and Synergistic Development
We have established a comprehensive management system that covers 
supplier access, cooperative process evaluation, and review and assessment,  
as part of our commitment to continuously enhancing supplier management  
standards. To ensure the fair evaluation of suppliers, we have established a  
fully transparent control mechanism in the supplier management process.  
We conduct comprehensive annual assessments of our cooperative suppliers  
every year, under which key indicators are systematically evaluated, including  
their q uality control capabilities, punctuality of delivery, service response levels, 
price competitiveness, and ESG performance. These assessments ensure that
  
supplier performance is comprehensively controllable.
To s trengthen the sustainable development orientation, the Company further 
optimized the audit mechanism in 2025. By dynamically tracking suppliers’ 
performance data in dimensions such as energy saving and emission  
reduction, social responsibility fulfillment, and compliance governance, we  
achieved quantitative assessment and process-oriented management of the  
ESG performance, thereby driving the overall supply chain towards a green,  
efficient, and responsible direction. In 2025, the supplier audit pass rate was  
90%.

Sustainable Supply Chain Development
The Group is committed to building a green and sustainable supply chain by  
actively guiding suppliers to improve environmental and safety management  
performance, ensuring their ESG risks remain within a controllable range,  
and comprehensively integrating sustainable development concepts into  
the supply chain management system. To this end, we incorporate ESG-
related assessment factors into the supplier access and evaluation process,  
and c omprehensively judge the standardisation and effectiveness of their ESG 
management through various methods such as document review, on-site  
inspection, background check and questionnaire assessment, to screen out  
partners with material ESG risks from the source.
In terms of evaluation aspects, we focus on suppliers ’ actual performance in  
environmental protection, pollution prevention and control, occupational  
health and safety, anti-corruption and business ethics, thereby systematically  
identifying their potential risks in compliant operations, environmental  
impacts, and information security. For suppliers with major negative ESG  
incidents identified during the performance evaluation or inspection reviews,  
we w ill take corresponding disciplinary measures according to regulations and 
continuously f ollow up on their rectification status to ensure that the issues are 
properly resolved.
Furthermore, we proactively communicate the Group ’s specific requirements  
and expectations in the ESG field to our suppliers, clearly guiding them to  
advance toward higher standards. When selecting partners, we prioritize  
suppliers that maintain good production environments and sustainable  
practice c apabilities, so as to overall reduce the supply chain ’s n egative impact 
on the environment and advance the realization of a more resilient and  
responsible supply chain system.
可持續供應鏈構建
本集團致力於打造綠色可持續供應鏈，積極
引導供應商提升環境與安全管理表現 ，確保
其 E SG 風險處於可控範圍，將可持續發展理念
全 面 融 入 供 應 鏈 管 理 體 系 。為 此，我 們 在 供

To further enhance supply chain resilience and anti-risk capabilities, and 
effectively respond to various risks that may cause disruptions on the supply  
chain such as extreme weather, unexpected events, and seasonal changes,  
especially climate change-induced supply chain disruption risks (including  
reduced production in raw material origins and interrupted transportation  
routes due to extreme weather, as well as unstable raw material supply  
caused by seasonal climate fluctuations), we have formulated refined, full-
process supply chain disruption risk management measures. We implement  
differentiated supply chain management measures according to three core  
aspects: new products, mature products, and single-raw-material products,  
based on the supply chain characteristics, raw material dependence, and  
market demand features of various products. By strengthening the supply  
chain structure, optimizing inventory management, and deepening supply  
chain c ooperation, we continue to identify and control supply chain disruption 
risks, e nsure the stable supply of product raw materials, and comprehensively 
enhance the overall anti-risk capabilities of the supply chain.

 ，有

Diverse Channels

In 2025, we continued to focus on the four main categories of Good Health  
by conducting precise penetration targeting their core demographic. On  
domestic e-commerce platforms, the collagen category ’s ranking in the joint  
health sector rose to second in the industry and firmly held the top spot in  
the collagen category. Oyster extract remained first in its category for three  
consecutive years, demonstrating our continuous leadership and brand  
influence in niche markets.

comprehensive e-commerce platforms (e.g., JD.com, Tmall), media platforms,  
and emerging content platforms (e.g., Douyin). E-commerce sales channels  
mainly included self-operated stores on JD.com, Tmall, Alibaba Health, and  
Douyin Live Streaming, accurately reaching different consumer groups through  
a m ulti-channel layout. During the year, we focused on deepening operations

The Group attaches great importance to the customer service experience. 
Focusing on an omni-channel online and offline layout, we implement  
differentiated service strategies based on the needs of customers in different  
channels, and fully enhance service quality and customer experience. Among  
them, for e-commerce platform customers, we proactively provide professional 
services during the pre-sales stage. We not only answer product-related  
questions in detail, but also offer personalized purchasing advice based on  
customer n eeds, accumulating a good reputation with professionalism. In the 
after-sales s tage, we strengthen response efficiency, comprehensively covering 
various c
ustomer needs such as logistics expediting, product usage guidance, 
and post-consumption feedback follow-up. This ensures timely resolution.
  
At the same time, regarding terminal customers corresponding to offline  
distributors, w e set up a dedicated consultation hotline to achieve immediate 
response a nd quick resolution of concerns, and systematically summarize and 
classify various issues fed back by customers, regularly synchronizing them to
  
marketing leaders to provide precise basis for product iteration and service  
optimization. This continues to improve our product and service quality.
To f urther optimize service experience and enhance customer satisfaction, we 
have established a comprehensive customer feedback analysis mechanism.  
Through data modeling analysis and customer feedback induction, we  
precisely c apture service shortcomings and continuously refine service details, 
providing a superior and more attentive full-process after-sales experience.
  
During the Reporting Period, we continued to strengthen service team  
building and improve team service quality and response efficiency. Service  
performance across various e-commerce platforms was outstanding: Tmall  
stores’ s ervice experience scores were all 4.81, self-operated stores on JD.com 
achieved all 103, and JD.com pop stores maintained all 9.8. Service levels across  
all channels met industry excellence benchmarks, fully reflecting the Group ’s 
investment and effectiveness in customer service.

Responsible Marketing
The Group always regards marketing compliance as an important cornerstone 
of corporate integrity. In strict compliance with the requirements of relevant  
laws a nd regulations, including the Advertising Law of the People’s R epublic of 
China, the Advertising Management Regulations , the Interim Provisions of the 
People’s Republic of China on Publishing Food Advertisements, and the Interim 
Provisions of the People ’s Republic of China on Censorship of Healthy Food  
Advertisements, we implement full-process supervision of marketing activities 
to ensure the truthfulness, legality, and compliance of advertisements.
To maintain a healthy and responsible consumer environment, the Group  
always adheres to the highest standards of truthfulness and accuracy in  
advertisements, t hereby firmly prohibiting any false or misleading information. 
Against t he backdrop of the rapid development of e-commerce live streaming, 
we s trictly adhere to the content review rules and management requirements 
formulated by the platform, implementing full-process compliance control
  
over scripts, product displays, and user interactions during the live streaming  
process. At the same time, we actively organize the team to participate in  
specialized training and study of platform rules, continuously enhancing our  
compliance r esponse capabilities for emerging marketing forms, ensuring that 
every live-streaming event not only complies with regulations and platform  
requirements b ut also provides consumers with a transparent, trustworthy, and 
reassuring shopping experience.
During the Reporting Period, we organized multiple dedicated training sessions  
focused o n key products to continuously strengthen the team ’s u nderstanding 
of product characteristics and professional application capabilities. By  
improving the accuracy and consistency of marketing content, we respond  
to consumer needs with a more responsible attitude, thereby ensuring that  
product communication is truthful, professional, and trustworthy.
負責任營銷
本集團始終將營銷合規視為企業誠信經營的
重要基石 ，嚴格遵循 《中華人民共和國廣告法》
《廣告管理條例 》 《食品廣告發佈暫行規定》 《保
健食品廣告審查暫行規定 》 等相關法律法規，
對營銷活動實施全流程監管 ，確保廣告宣傳
的真實性 、合法性與合規性。
為 維 護 健 康 、負 責 任 的 消 費 環 境，本 集 團 在
廣告宣傳中始終堅持最高標準的真實性與準
確 性 ，堅 決 杜 絕 任 何 虛 假 或 誤 導 性 信 息。在
電商直播快速發展的背景下 ，我們嚴格遵循
平台制定的內容審核規則與管理要求 ，對直
播過程中的話術 、產品展示及用戶互動實施
全 流 程 合 規 把 控 。同 時，我 們 積 極 組 織 團 隊
參與平台規則的專項培訓與學習 ，持續提升
對新興營銷形式的合規應對能力 ，確保每一
場直播不僅符合法規與平台要求 ，更能為消
費者帶來透明 、可信、安心的購物體驗。
報告期內 ，我們圍繞主推產品組織多場專項
培訓
，持續強化團隊對產品特性的理解與專

 。通過提升營銷內容的準確性與

ADVANCING TOGETHER WITH EMPLOYEES TO FOSTER 
SYNERGY
Nanjing Sinolife is committed to achieving synergistic development between 
employees and the Company. We fully recognize that our workforce is  
the cornerstone for our stable long-term development. We systematically  
promote talent team building around three strategic directions: “platform-
driven attraction, growth-led incentivization, and culture-based cohesion ”. 
Persisting in the principle of mutual respect, we have built a comprehensive  
support system covering rights and interests protection, career development,  
and health and safety. We not only create an equal and safe working and  
living e nvironment for our employees, but also foster team cohesion through 
continuous attention and heartwarming care, empowering employees to
  
realize t heir personal value while growing together with the Company towards 
a shared future.
Employee Rights and Interests
Nanjing Sinolife considers its employees as the most valuable asset 
for corporate development. We remain committed to respecting and
  
safeguarding the rights and interests of all employees, and abide by  
responsible labor standards. The Group strictly complies with relevant labor  
laws a nd regulations in the places where it operates, including the Labour Law 
of the People ’s Republic of China , the Labour Contract Law of the People ’s 
Republic o f China , the New Zealand Human Rights Act 1993 , the New Zealand 
Employment R elations Act 2005 , and the New Zealand Holidays Act 2003 . We 
have formulated and continuously improved internal regulations including  
the Employee Recruitment System and Remuneration Management System  
to ensure standardized employee management and the full protection of  
employees’ rights and interests. In the onboarding process, we enter into the  
Induction Notice with new recruits, and updated the induction details during  
the Reporting Period, to assist employees in having a full understanding of  
company policies, culture, and relevant rights and interests, facilitating their  
quick integration into the team and clarifying their career development  
direction.

重要基石

Labor Practices
Nanjing Sinolife strictly complies with internationally recognized labor 
standards across all operating locations, and resolutely prohibits any form  
of forced labor and child labor. To eliminate child labor at its source, we  
have established a strict recruitment review mechanism. In the onboarding  
process, we strictly verify the identity documents of all candidates to ensure  
that t heir actual age meets the local statutory employment age requirements. 
At the same time, we advocate for and enforce a compliant working hours
  
management system, ensuring that all work performed by employees is  
entirely v oluntary. We protect the rights of our employees to rest and leave in 
accordance w
 ith the law and resolutely resist any form of forced or compulsory 
labor.
During the Reporting Period, Nanjing Sinolife did not experience any non-
compliance incidents involving the use of child labor and forced labor.
Diversity and Inclusion
In the recruitment and career development of employees, we actively 
implement diverse recruitment principles and adamantly oppose any  
discrimination based on age, citizenship, marital status, race, skin color,  
ethnicity, religious beliefs, physical condition, gender identity, and other  
factors. Furthermore, we adopt a zero-tolerance policy towards all forms of  
harassment, and address relevant non-compliance incidents proactively and  
with the utmost seriousness.
勞工實踐
南京中生在所有營運地嚴格遵守國際公認的
勞工準則
，堅決禁止任何形式的強迫勞動與
童 工
。為 從 源 頭 上 杜 絕 童 工 現 象，我 們 建 立
了 嚴 格 的 招 聘 審 查 機 制
 。在 入 職 流 程 中，我
們嚴格核驗所有候選人的身份證明文件 ，確
保其實際年齡符合當地法定就業年齡的要求 。
同時 ，我們倡導並執行合規的工時管理制度，
確保所有員工的工作完全出於自願
 ，並保障
其依法享有休息休假權利 ，堅決抵制任何形
式的強迫或強制勞動行為 。
報告期內 ，南京中生未發生任何涉及使用童
工及強迫勞動的違規事件
 。
多元共融
在員工招聘及職業發展過程中，我們積極踐
行多元化招聘原則
 ，堅決反對因年齡、國籍、
婚姻狀況 、種族、膚色、民族、宗教信仰、身
體狀況或性別認同等因素產生的任何歧視行
為，並對任何形式的騷擾行為秉持零容忍態
度，積極
、嚴肅處理相關違規事件。

Talent Recruitment
The Group has continually expanded its diverse recruitment models, 
continuously injecting new vitality into the team and promoting continuous  
optimization of the talent structure through a combination of channels  
including new media, campus recruitment, human resource markets, and  
internal referrals. Our internal referral program not only effectively attracts  
highly compatible talent but also significantly enhances employees ’ sense of  
belonging. W e also encourage employees to share job postings through their 
Moments, leveraging their personal connections to maximize job visibility.  
By implementing targeted outreach strategies tailored to each role ’s unique  
requirements, we ensure recruitment messages reach the specific groups  
precisely, further enhancing recruitment efficiency. This approach not only  
reduces the costs associated with traditional channels but also showcases  
the Company ’s culture and values, attracting talent whose beliefs align with  
ours. At the digital recruitment level, we leverage professional platforms such  
as BOSS Zhipin and Liepin to establish a direct and efficient communication  
mechanism with candidates, enhancing the p recision of our screening process. 
In addition, we actively participate in campus recruitment to precisely replenish  
our workforce with fresh talent while continuously strengthening our employer  
brand.
During the Reporting Period, targeting the two major groups of fresh forces  
and high-caliber and specialized talent, the Group actively participated  
in various recruitment activities organized by government departments.  
Leveraging the precise outreach advantage of official platforms, the  
Company effectively introduced high-quality talent aligned with its strategic  
development needs, injecting sustained momentum into the team.

參與校園招聘
In March 2025, Nanjing Sinolife actively participated in the “Staying in Nanjing, Win-win Future ” 2025 Spring Tour Recruitment Fair  
(Nanjing Normal University Station) organized by government departments. Through on-site presentations and in-depth interactions,  
we s uccessfully built a bridge for talent matching between the university and the enterprise. The event collected nearly 100 resumes, 
with p ositive outcomes in interview conversion and new-hire placements. Through the following measures, we achieved significant 
progress in deepening university-enterprise collaboration and strengthening our employer brand.
2025 年 3 月
，南京中生積極參與由政府部門主辦的 「就在南京，共贏未來」 2025 春季巡迴招聘會 （南京師範大學站） ，

covered fresh graduates and prospective graduates in new media, e-commerce and other related majors, effectively  
shortening the talent screening path and improving the job-candidate alignment from the source.

群體 ，有效縮短人才篩選路徑，從源頭提升人崗匹配度。

culture, d evelopment prospects, and position benefits, helping students develop concrete understanding of the organization

platforms, we further enhanced our brand appeal to high-level talent, injecting new momentum into the Company ’s long-term
 
development.

能力與核心競爭力

Remuneration and Incentives
The Group has established a holistic remuneration and performance  
management system, and continues to optimize its remuneration and  
performance management policies. By closely aligning employee growth  
with our business development, we strive to achieve a mutually beneficial  
dynamic for both the Company and its employees. We continue to refine  
our assessment and incentive mechanisms, as part of our efforts to build a  
diversified and comprehensive incentive system that combines materials,  
spiritual, and target incentives. Our system and policies effectively ignite the  
enthusiasm of our employees, enhance organizational efficiency, and fuel our  
sustained business development.
薪酬激勵
本集團建立了完善的薪酬績效管理體系並持

 ，將員工個人發展

 ，提 升 組 織 效 能，推 動 企 業

In t erms of remuneration structure, we implemented a dynamic performance-
based r
emuneration system, where employees receive monthly compensation

supplements ” not only effectively boosts the team ’s execution capabilities

between talent value and business development.

The Group is committed to establishing an employee communication 
mechanism that equally emphasizes normalization and institutionalization,  
ensuring unobstructed channels for opinion expression and continuously  
enhancing employee satisfaction. In our daily operations, we encourage  
employees to provide feedback to their superiors at any time through  
telephone, instant messaging, or emails to achieve immediate interaction.  
At the same time, we review work progress and plan future directions on an  
ad-hoc basis, guided by project requirements and departmental feedback.  
To systematically listen to the voices of our employees, we have designated  
the 1st of each month as a fixed “Opinion Collection Day ”. Employees can  
provide their suggestions on aspects such as company management,  
working conditions, and fringe benefit package. Upon consolidation, the  
human resources department conducts categorical assessments and  
follow-up actions to ensure that constructive suggestions are effectively  
implemented. I n addition, we regularly assess the operational effectiveness of 
our communication mechanism and pursue continuous optimization based  
on the assessment results. We also strengthen employees’  communication  
skills through training covering listening, expression, and non-verbal  
communication, comprehensively enhancing organizational communication  
efficiency. We firmly believe that by establishing a respectful, transparent,  
and efficient communication system, we not only ensure that employees  
feel valued but also unleash their potential to jointly drive the Group ’s robust  
development.
During the Reporting Period, Sinolife United established a dedicated Human  
Resources Business Partner (HRBP) group to enhance the efficiency and  
precision of issue resolution. The HRBP group categorizes collected information  
and delivers targeted communications through various channels, ensuring  
communication r eaches the relevant parties directly. Meanwhile, Good Health 
offi cially established a labor union in September this year, serving as a two-way, 
neutral, and systematic communication bridge between employees and the  
Company. Through these initiatives, we have effectively addressed traditional  
communication pain points such as “employees ’ hesitation to speak, the  
company’s inability to hear, information asymmetry, and lack of follow-up on  
feedback”.
